# Errors on PO files


## Error with polib
We use the po metadata to get the language code and the team, but sometimes it fails, here are the files.

| Package | Lang from file path/name | polib error | Team |
|---------|--------------------------|-------------|------|
 | [tidy]({{< ref "/f34/package/tidy.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f34/tidy/localize/translations/language_pt_br.po | error-os |  | 
 | [torbrowser-launcher]({{< ref "/f34/package/torbrowser-launcher.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f34/torbrowser-launcher/po/pt_BR.po | error-os |  | 
 | [tuxmath]({{< ref "/f34/package/tuxmath.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f34/tuxmath/po/pt_BR.po | error-unicode |  | 
{.sortable}



## Error with calcstats

We use the calcstats from translate toolkit to get the translation progress, but sometimes it fails, here are the files:

| Package | Lang from file path/name | Team |
|---------|--------------------------|------|
 | [torbrowser-launcher]({{< ref "/f34/package/torbrowser-launcher.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f34/torbrowser-launcher/po/pt_BR.po |  |
